# Whole-Registry Contract Review (`R2`)

# Stage 4 Whole-Registry Contract Review — WHOLE_REGISTRY

**Target:** `/Volumes/SSD_1TB_M2/work/wt/local-fsm-ro-2026-11-18/mujoco-0502660d` @ `0502660d` (read-only, clean tree). **Scope:** the 56 R1-reconciled properties evaluated as ONE contract across native CPU drivetrain, GPU kernels, configuration/wire validation, runtime stepping, recording/replay, rider policies, batch runners, and verification tooling. No repository files were modified.

## Verification posture

Load-bearing claims were checked against source where the property named a mechanism. **Verified consistent:** pedaling FSM step logic (pedaling.cpp:147-253), stall detector once-per-window semantics (stall_detector.hpp), `request_crank_reposition` dual gate (runtime.cpp:830-857), brake resolution split caller/rollback/rider (step.cpp:2394-2417), balance-monitor grace/braked-stop rules (control.cpp:296-317 + step.cpp:2803-2809 — matches P09c exactly), `advance_control` delivery-pending idempotence and bounded loop (research.cpp:341-395), `finish()` idempotence + `finalization_attempted_` retry structure (lifecycle.cpp:202-260), crash-atomic publish with manifest-link-last ordering (publish.cpp:43-107), `operator_stop_deferred` journal ordering vs `OperatorInterrupted` (lifecycle.cpp:240-245, interrupt.cpp), `StaticBrake` latch/reseat/atomic restore (static_brake.cpp), `PlaybackClock` defined-but-uncalled (lifecycle.cpp:263-292, declared policy_session.hpp:58). **Verified gaps:** P01b boundary coverage, P02a/P03a set_state cross-field checks, P03c hold coherence, P07b `peek_rollback_brake_demand` (absent), P07a probe model purity (absent — dof_frictionloss written, never restored), P04b split cut-window (present), P11(b) stale `stop_requested` (present). **Not fully traced:** transmission identity across every reset/prepare/set_ratio path (P01c — structure at transmission.cpp:365-464 supports it), GPU kernels, P12-P13 init-cache paths, P16(d) orphan-episode sealing, P18 halt-frame rollback, P20-P22 OS/solver specifics, P23 tire-writer RTSan paths, P24 sweep tooling — treated as unverified rather than violated.

## Principal cross-property conflicts

1. **P01b**: `reposition_on_stall` is gated on `motor_clutch` only at runtime (`request_crank_reposition`), NOT at `validate` (checks `pedal_attachment` only) or wire decode — an admissible config can arm a stall reflex that backdrives a rigid rotor, while the manual path refuses the same setup. Asymmetric, not just incomplete.
2. **P02a/P03a vs `set_state`**: relational invariants (`goal⇔reason∈{1,2}`, `goal⊥cooldown`, `goal⇒!coasting&&effort==0`, `!stall_elapsed⇒accumulators==0`, reject `coasting/goal && effort!=0`) are absent from both `decode_policies` and `PedalingPolicy::set_state` — invariant-violating snapshots are stageable.
3. **P03c vs `AssistController::set_state`**: hold coherence checks absent — `release_hold_left_s>0` with zero `hold_power_w`/`hold_torque_nm` admitted; step then interprets as degenerate zero-cap rather than corrupt state.
4. **P07b names machinery that doesn't exist**: no `peek_rollback_brake_demand`; the `resolved==nullptr` probe path passes `rollback_demand=0` unconditionally → probe/advance divergence under engaged rollback hold.
5. **P07a probe purity**: probe path writes `dof_frictionloss` via `brake_.apply` and can flip `posvel_fresh_` — no snapshot/restore found; purity holds only because every advance rewrites bounds first.
6. **P04b confirmed live**: `cut_remaining_s > 0.` (writers ×3) vs `> 1e-12` (step/launch) — the exact `(0,1e-12]` split window the property condemns.
7. **P11(b) confirmed live**: `handle_failure` leaves `stop_requested` set; `acknowledge_control` unreachable post-failure → `status()` reports stale flag on terminated episode.
8. **P22(c-2) confirmed**: `drivetrain::validate` omits the brake-domain checks both `post_init` and `decode_drive` enforce.

## End-to-end progress/recovery gaps

- **Unbounded reposition retry lattice**: `reposition_on_stall` re-arms the stall detector on every disengage; a persistent mechanical stall cycles stall→goal→timeout→cooldown forever with only indirect balance/crash termination — no `max_reposition_attempts` exists.
- **Grace-burn asymmetry**: braked standstill before grace expiry does NOT re-arm (needs `hold` phase or spent grace); the same standstill one tick later does — a rider stopped mid-grace gets `balance_lost` sooner than one stopped after.
- **Queued reposition lost on restore**: `pending_reposition_` cleared by design — operator intent doesn't survive checkpointing and escapes the replay evidence battery entirely (it's not a `rider_command`).
- **Probe reads weaker brakes than advance will apply** under engaged rollback hold (see conflicts 4-5).

## Missing product requirements

- Unified `reposition_possible` predicate (validate/decode/runtime disagree).
- Reposition retry bound / 'drivetrain jammed' terminal condition.
- Assist-hold incoherence semantics (reject vs degenerate-zero) undeclared.
- `PlaybackClock` dead-vs-live decision unrecorded; no marker found despite being unreferenced outside its definition.
- `operator_stop_deferred` scope (research-only?) implicit, not written.
- `-6 N` rider-support rule scope (accounting vs acceptance-gating) unresolved.
- GPU↔CPU numerical parity criterion absent — batch machinery is specified without the comparison contract it serves.

## Over-anchoring/formulation biases

- **ULP-precision liveness** (P02b): `timeout + 0.5·ulp` mis-bounds a strict `>` comparison — the true bound is `timeout + dt`.
- **Oracle-parity constants as contract**: `1e-12`/`1e-9` sentinels, `same_bits` comparisons, expression-order-as-arithmetic, `max_offer=1e6`, `max(.05·scale, timestep)` — engineering details presented as invariants (P03b honestly flags one instance; the pattern recurs).
- **Bitwise-identity as product requirement** (P15c, P18 `executable_sha256`): appropriate same-environment engineering gates, but absolute enough to punish legitimate toolchain changes with no behavioral diff.
- **Representation-anchored invariants** (P02a a'): accumulators-must-be-zero-when-unarmed encodes this representation, not the behavior ('no phantom progress on re-arm' — already guaranteed by `stall_update`).
- **`1e-9` s clock tolerance** (P08d) at 1.25 ms timesteps — precision without physical meaning.

## Recommendations (priority order)

1. Close the P01b boundary gap: enforce `reposition_on_stall ⇒ motor_clutch` at `validate` + decode, or document the runtime-only trust boundary explicitly.
2. Add the relational cross-field checks to `PedalingPolicy::set_state`/`decode_policies` (P02a/P03a) and `AssistController::set_state` (P03c) — the advancing step already assumes them.
3. Either implement `peek_rollback_brake_demand` per P07b or weaken the property to 'probe ignores rollback' with a documented rationale; and add probe-side save/restore of `dof_frictionloss`/`posvel_fresh_` per P07a.
4. Land the single `shift_cut_active` predicate P04b already prescribes — the split window is small but exactly measurable.
5. Clear `stop_requested` in `handle_failure` (P11b's named defect) and bound automatic stall-repositions or escalate 'jammed' as terminal.
6. Relax P02b's ULP bound to `timeout + dt` (or assert `elapsed` never lands exactly on timeout by construction); mark P14d's PlaybackClock dead or wire it; state the GPU↔CPU parity criterion explicitly.

## Bottom line

As a combined contract, the registry is largely faithful to the code — most load-bearing semantics (pedaling FSM, brake resolution, balance monitor, research lifecycle, crash-atomic publish, deferred-SIGINT journaling) verify cleanly. The real weaknesses cluster at **validation boundaries** (validate vs decode vs runtime disagree on reposition gating; set_state boundaries admit invariant-violating snapshots the step logic assumes impossible) and **probe purity** (P07a/P07b over-claim non-interference: the probe writes model state and drops the rollback demand). No outright logical contradictions inside the registry itself — the conflicts are properties over-claiming vs. code, plus four open product decisions the registry honestly tracks.

# Stage 4 (`R2`) Adversarial Audit Report — `P01a`: Lossless Drivetrain & Unilateral Clutch Invariant

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Adversarial Compliance Verdict (`R2`):** `partially_violated`
- **Adversarial Justification Verdict (`R2`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Compliance Challenge
Probed all four conjuncts across the CPU soft-limit transmission mechanism, DrivetrainWriter stage/commit/restore paths, the energy-ledger seam, and the GPU kernel. (a) Loss: PhysicalStep::loss_increment sums a fixed source list (fork_damper, shock_damper, shock_hbo, engine_passive, front/rear_static_brake, brake_hold, tires, rider_contacts, shock_top_out/upper_stop; native/src/runtime/step.cpp:2711-2786) with an explicit comment that one-way clutch engagement is tendon-limit solver work charged to the closure channel, never a loss (step.cpp:2743-2745). numerical_constraint_powers emits 'tendon_limit:{efc_id}' powers (step.cpp:917-966) which raw_work routes to closure_signed/absolute_j (native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:83-99), and the drivetrain constraint span is published as components['ideal_transmission'] after subtraction from joint_limits (step.cpp:1859-1867) - no drivetrain component reaches the loss list. (b) Sign: crank_clutch is a fixed joint tendon L=crank-shaft declared with range[-1e12,0] (native/src/model/physical_topology.cpp:12-13,121-122); only the upper limit can engage. stage_solved_into sums mjCNSTR_LIMIT_TENDON rows for the tendon (native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:497-505) and mulJacTVec maps the positive row force through J=[+1,-1] to +lambda on the driven dof, so the clutch's own span at drive_shaft_spin is >= 0; crank_clutch_torque_nm reads exactly that span (native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:1119-1126). Under coherent reachable state no lower-limit row exists, so (b) holds. (c) Open-when-unloaded: a slack tendon produces no efc row, giving tension=0, force_=0, torque=0 (transmission.cpp:483-506); the committed bound ratchets to min(boundary,phi) on every advancing tick (transmission.cpp:328-344). Holds. (d) Run-on: AssistController::step arms hold_power_w/hold_torque_nm/release_hold_left_s when the target drops below delivered torque with omega>0 (native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:81-86) and delivers min(hold_power_w/omega, hold_torque_nm, limit) through the window (motor.cpp:104-108); the mid_drive actuator drives drive_shaft_spin with ctrllimited ctrlrange 0..max (physical_topology.cpp:114-125) and the shaft-to-ideal_hub-to-rear_wheel path is the same unilateral tendon (motor_clutch.cpp:8-16; drivetrain.cpp:843-875,1095-1131). Holds while the shaft turns forward. HOWEVER the protections are model convention, not admission contract: (i) restore() admits any finite range[0] - validate_state checks finiteness only (transmission.cpp:610-623) - which makes the lower-limit row physically reachable and produces negative shaft torque through a validated state; (ii) topology::joint/scalar_joint/actuator never check dof_damping, dof_frictionloss, jnt_stiffness, tendon_stiffness, tendon_damping or tendon_frictionloss (native/src/model_topology.hpp:10-36; Transmission ctor checks only wrap_type==mjWRAP_JOINT and coefficient count, transmission.cpp:111-139), so an admitted model can place viscous drivetrain dissipation into qfrc_passive -> engine_passive -> loss_j (step.cpp:1883-1884,2721-2730) or into FRICTION_* rows skipped by both constraint observers (step.cpp:884-899,934-949); (iii) the GPU kernel has no drive_shaft, crank_clutch, motor_clutch or assist representation at all (native/src/gpu/drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp:22-54) - the unilateral-clutch conjuncts are unexercised on the GPU leg and no loss accounting exists in batch rollouts. Statement-precision defect: 'f[drive_shaft_spin] >= 0' is true only for the clutch's own solve span; the aggregate transmission_ span at the shaft dof is legitimately negative whenever the freehub reaction -lambda*ratio resists motor drive (drivetrain.cpp:1095-1127).

## 2. Concrete Counterexamples / Repros
R1 (violates (b)+(c) via validated restore): call DrivetrainWriter::restore with clutch={ratio:1.,rear_teeth:3,coefficients:[1.],range:[0.,0.],boundary:0.,shift_pending:false,shift_parameter_work_j:0.,shift_constraint_work_j:0.,last_tension_n:0.}. It passes validate (ratio/teeth/coefficients pinned at native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:48-52; finiteness-only checks at native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:610-623; restore wiring drivetrain.cpp:1234-1251 -> transmission.cpp:642-657 -> commit writes update.range into model tendon_range at transmission.cpp:419-423). Then set qvel[crank_spin]=-2, qvel[drive_shaft_spin]=0 and advance: phi=crank-shaft falls below 0, the LOWER limit row activates, efc_force>0 on the negated Jacobian gives force_[drive_shaft_dof]=-lambda<0 - retarding shaft torque while 'unloaded'. No test mutates range or boundary; test_drive_restore_rejects_auxiliary_transmission_drift covers only ratio/rear_teeth/coefficients (native/tests/reference/test_native_state_restore.cpp:808-841). R2 (violates (a) via admitted model): build an mjModel with damping or frictionloss on crank_spin/drive_shaft_spin/rear_wheel_spin, or tendon_damping/tendon_frictionloss on crank_clutch/ideal_mid_drive_freehub - topology gates accept it (native/src/model_topology.hpp:10-36; transmission.cpp:111-139). Viscous damping folds into d->qfrc_passive -> components['engine_passive'] (native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1883-1884) -> engine_passive_loss_power -> loss += power*dt (step.cpp:2721-2730): literal viscous drivetrain dissipation in loss_j. Dry friction creates mjCNSTR_FRICTION_DOF/FRICTION_TENDON rows skipped by both switches (step.cpp:884-899,934-949) so the dissipation is invisible to loss AND closure and is absorbed silently into residual_j (native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:190-191). The repo's own fixture declares crank_clutch range="-100 0" (native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:1800-1803): the bilateral lower bound is physically reachable once |crank-shaft|>100 rad. R3 (incoherent restore, energy injection / dead zone): restore clutch={boundary:+1e6} or {boundary:-1e6,range:[-1e12,-1e6]}; validate admits any finite boundary (transmission.cpp:614-623; boundary may even be absent, finite_optional at transmission.cpp:614). Next stage_prepare_into writes range[1]=min(boundary,phi) (transmission.cpp:328-344): a far-below boundary yields a ~1e6-rad spring violation (huge +lambda, energy injection through the closure channel); a far-above boundary wedges the clutch open while coherence requires engagement. R4 (GPU): GpuDrivetrainParams{cog_teeth:0.f} -> gpu_drivetrain_ratio silently returns 1.0f (native/src/gpu/drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp:57-61) where the CPU rejects teeth<3 (native/src/config_validation.hpp:113-115); chainring_teeth<=0 gives ratio<=0 and zeroes rider_wheel_drive_nm (kernel:219). GpuDrivetrainParams/State/Output contain no shaft/clutch/motor fields (kernel:22-54); include_rear_drive_torque merely toggles analytic-pawl vs tendon mode (kernel:289-327, native/src/gpu/batch_step.hpp:187-220). R5 (boundary values, all admitted, no violation): release_hold_s=0 passes the nonnegative gate (config_validation.hpp:182) but the window can never arm (motor.cpp:82-86); omega<=0 during a hold yields delivery=0 (motor.cpp:106); a restored hold_power_w>0 with torque=0 is cleared by the else branch (motor.cpp:87-91); a restored release_hold_left_s=1e9 / hold_torque_nm=1e9 stays armed indefinitely though delivery is still bounded by limit/cap (motor.cpp:104-108) and ctrlrange (physical_topology.cpp:125). NaN/+Inf/-Inf: the wire sentinel decodes through real() (native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:98-125) but is rejected by finite_real and the finite/nonnegative/positive/finite_optional gates at decode and validate (state_codec.cpp:126-136,176-178; transmission.cpp:610-623; native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:28-35,53-58; drivetrain.cpp:600-601,763-774,1221-1232; native/src/validation.hpp:10-29) - no NaN/Inf hole found on the drivetrain restore path.

## 3. Necessity Challenge (Blocked Valid Scenarios)
None found - the constraint restates the confirmed product contract rather than blocking any valid scenario. Decision 20 requires 100% efficiency with zero losses in sprockets, cassette, hub, freewheels, bearings and all other drivetrain mechanics (docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:210-218; AGENTS.md section Передача). Decision 43 fixes exactly two freewheels, the unilateral crank_clutch that opens freely on backward crank, motor drive of drive_shaft while pedals are stationary or turning backward, and clutch solver work booked to the elastic channel rather than losses (docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:471-489). Decision 44 and RIDE.md:178-186 require the motor clutch and rear freehub to decouple the drive during backward crank repositioning so the wheel and motor shaft are never backdriven (simulation-decisions.md:490-519). A retarding or residual clutch torque would itself violate decision 43; engine braking or regeneration through the drivetrain is excluded by the real overrunning-clutch model, and slope holding is assigned to rider brakes (decision 19, simulation-decisions.md:208-209). No scenario in simulation-decisions.md, RIDE.md or RECORDING.md (no drivetrain/clutch/loss terms present there) requires what the property forbids. The only over-reach is the GPU leg: the batch GPU rollout cannot express motor_clutch at all, so the constraint is vacuous there rather than wrongly blocking anything.

## 4. Sufficiency Challenge (Admitted Invalid Scenarios)
Even where the forward-execution invariant holds, the admission boundary accepts states that break or corrupt it: (1) finite or inverted clutch range and boundary/range/phi divergence are all admitted through restore (transmission.cpp:610-623,642-657; drivetrain.cpp:48-52) - the exact states that produce negative shaft torque, dead zones or ~1e6-rad spring preload energy injection. (2) dof_damping, dof_frictionloss, jnt_stiffness, dof_armature on drivetrain joints and tendon_stiffness/tendon_damping/tendon_frictionloss on drivetrain tendons are never checked by topology::joint/scalar_joint/actuator or the Transmission ctor (native/src/model_topology.hpp:10-36; transmission.cpp:111-139): damping reaches loss_j via engine_passive (native/src/runtime/step.cpp:2721-2730), dry friction vanishes into residual_j unaccounted (accounting.cpp:190-191) - dissipation that either lands in the wrong channel or in no channel. (3) GPU parameters are unvalidated: cog_teeth<=0 silently becomes ratio 1.0f and chainring_teeth<=0 silently zeroes drive (native/src/gpu/drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp:57-61,219) versus the CPU teeth>=3 gate (native/src/config_validation.hpp:113-115). (4) AssistSnapshot.release_hold_left_s, hold_power_w and hold_torque_nm are unbounded above - only nonnegative is checked (native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:28-35) - so a restore arms a run-on hold the live FSM cannot produce (bounded only by the actuator ceiling during delivery). (5) control.human_torque_nm has no nonnegative gate at the tick boundary (native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:678): a negative external human command produces negative human_crank actuator torque on the unclamped human_crank actuator (native/src/model/physical_topology.cpp:127), an admitted 'foot pulls the pedal' violation upstream of the clutch (it opens the clutch rather than backdriving the shaft, so the drivetrain invariant survives while the rider model is corrupted). (6) Snapshot coherence vs model state is unchecked: angles count/finiteness only (drivetrain.cpp:1223-1228), last_time_s finite only (drivetrain.cpp:1221) - a restored future last_time_s wedges every advance ('drivetrain state can advance only once per timestamp', drivetrain.cpp:600-602,772-774) until restart_clock; s.range[1] need not equal s.boundary and pending_actuation fields are individually but not relationally checked (drivetrain.cpp:1229-1233). (7) commit(SolvedTransmission&) swaps solved.state including last_tension_n and diagnostics without validation (transmission.cpp:555-565), so corrupt (NaN) efc_force would poison live state - a pre-corrupt-mjData case at the assumption boundary. (8) Probe (non-advancing) ticks still write detached-candidate torques into live data_->ctrl (drivetrain.cpp:973-986) - documented behavior, but a stale motor torque can sit on the actuator until a later settle ceiling check catches a mismatch (drivetrain.cpp:1053-1063).

## 5. Proposed Contract Revision
Restate per representation and per constraint row. (1) CPU unilateral clause: 'for each drivetrain one-way tendon T in {ideal_mid_drive_freehub, geometric_mid_drive_freehub, crank_clutch}, every mjCNSTR_LIMIT_TENDON efc row for T carries efc_force >= 0 and contributes -lambda*J generalized force, so T's own solve span at its driven DOF is >= 0, and when L(T) is below the committed upper bound no row exists and T's torque is exactly 0' - explicitly per-transmission, since the aggregate span at drive_shaft_spin is legitimately negative under freehub reaction (native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:1095-1127). (2) Loss clause: keep the channel claim but enforce its hidden premise at admission - dof_damping=dof_frictionloss=jnt_stiffness=dof_armature=0 on crank_spin/drive_shaft_spin/rear_wheel_spin/pedal_*_spin and tendon_stiffness=tendon_damping=tendon_frictionloss=0 on drivetrain tendons, checked in topology::joint/scalar_joint and the Transmission ctor (today unchecked: native/src/model_topology.hpp:10-36; native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:111-139). (3) Restore coherence: extend Transmission::validate_state to require range[0]<=range[1], range[0] equal to the declared unilateral bound (or below any reachable tendon length), and boundary<=range[1] when present, plus a boundary<=relative(qpos) cross-check at writer restore (transmission.cpp:610-623; drivetrain.cpp:1213-1251); bound AssistSnapshot.release_hold_left_s<=config.release_hold_s and hold_torque_nm<=config.max_torque in set_state (native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:28-35). (4) GPU leg: restate explicitly as 'analytic unilateral freehub spring (rear_drive_torque_nm>=0, crank reaction <=0) with no loss or closure accounting; drive_shaft/crank_clutch/motor are unrepresented' and add intake validation chainring_teeth>0 && cog_teeth>=3 instead of the silent ratio=1.0f fallback (native/src/gpu/drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp:57-61). (5) Operationalize 'open/engaged' as absence/presence of a mjCNSTR_LIMIT_TENDON row for the tendon (transmission.cpp:497-505); document crank_clutch_engaged's >1e-8 threshold as telemetry, not the physical predicate (drivetrain.cpp:1123-1126).

# Stage 4 (`R2`) Adversarial Audit Report — `P01b`: Autonomous Stall-Reposition Motor-Clutch Config Guard

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Adversarial Compliance Verdict (`R2`):** `violated`
- **Adversarial Justification Verdict (`R2`):** `relevant_engineering_contract`

## 1. Compliance Challenge
The claimed guard does not exist at either boundary. (a) bike::configuration::validate -> post_init(SimulationPhysicsConfig&) contains exactly ONE reposition_on_stall cross-check: native/src/configuration/validate.cpp:1330-1331 gates it on articulated.pedal_attachment == "spindle", never on drive.motor_clutch. The only motor_clutch rule in the same function (validate.cpp:1343-1346) is the converse gate: motor_clutch==true requires an effort drive mode. (b) The wire boundary bike::configuration::wire::validate_config (native/src/configuration/schema.cpp:1099-1262) performs no reposition_on_stall cross-check at all: it checks rider_contacts.pedal_attachment membership in {flat,weld,spindle} (schema.cpp:1072-1073) and drive.drive_mode/transmission_model domains (schema.cpp:1244-1254), but never joins drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall to either field. (c) The typed wire decode nativecfg::decode -> decode_drive (native/src/writer_config.hpp:962, 1067-1113) carries the same hole: its only cross-check (writer_config.hpp:1109-1111) forbids clutch/rotor on passive modes. (d) drivetrain::validate(DriveConfig) (native/src/config_validation.hpp:207-220) and DrivetrainWriter::checked_config (native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:19-22) add nothing. The ONLY place motor_clutch is enforced for the manoeuvre is the manual path NativeRideRuntime::request_crank_reposition (native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:848-855), which requires pedal_attachment=="spindle" AND drive().config().motor_clutch - a conjunction, not the clutch alone. Meanwhile the autonomous trigger PedalingPolicy::update (native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:82-125) reads only config_.reposition_on_stall; PedalingConfig has no motor_clutch member, so the policy cannot even see the topology it violates.

## 2. Concrete Counterexamples / Repros
Reachable admission at every named boundary: SimulationPhysicsConfig{physics_mode="physical", drive_mode="crank_effort" or "articulated_effort", articulated.pedal_attachment="spindle", drive.motor_clutch=false, drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall=true} passes post_init (validate.cpp:1330 sees only the spindle, satisfied; 1343 skipped since clutch=false), passes project_drive_config/project_writer_config -> wire::validate_config (project_config.cpp:392-405), and passes nativecfg::decode (writer_config.hpp:1067-1113). NativeRideRuntime constructs fine with motor_clutch=false - proven by the shipped test that builds reposition_env with p.drive.motor_clutch=false and only fails later inside request_crank_reposition (native/tests/reference/test_crank_reposition.cpp:565-580). In that runtime the stall reflex is live: stage_pedal_advance calls pedaling.update unconditionally (writers/drivetrain.cpp:753-758) with enabled=in.active && rider_enabled && effort_ (drivetrain.cpp:679); on a loaded stall (effort >= reposition_min_effort_nm) the reflex_edge fires (pedaling.cpp:91-100) and commands target_rate<0 on a topology where crank is rigidly bound to the motor shaft: motor_clutch.cpp:11-14 maps actuator_joint=reduction_driver="crank_spin", reduction_body="crank" with no drive_shaft and no crank_clutch, and drivetrain.cpp:135-138 folds shaft_joint_ onto crank_joint_. The manoeuvre therefore back-drives the motor - precisely what decision #44 cites the clutch for (simulation-decisions.md:499-505) and what the test comment states: 'Without the clutch, backpedal would drag the motor backward' (test_crank_reposition.cpp:538-539). Wire-side repro: a wire doc {schema:2, drive:{motor_clutch:false, pedaling:{reposition_on_stall:true,...}}, rider_contacts:{pedal_attachment:"flat",...}} passes wire::validate_config - a combination the typed layer rejects - so the two boundaries disagree. Reverse-direction mismatch also holds: {reposition_on_stall:true, motor_clutch:true, pedal_attachment:"flat"} is REJECTED (validate.cpp:1330) although the stated property would admit it.

## 3. Necessity Challenge (Blocked Valid Scenarios)
No confirmed valid scenario is blocked by the intended guard. simulation-decisions.md:487-488 retains motor_clutch=false only 'in modes without pedaling and in regression comparison tests'; the stall reflex is a pedaling behaviour, outside that envelope. Ruling #44 (simulation-decisions.md:498-500, and commit f3fa770d cited there) fixes the manoeuvre's preconditions as spindle AND motor_clutch, and the runtime enforces both for the manual request of the identical manoeuvre. Physically a rigid link can still backpedal (the rear freehub still freewheels; only the motor rotor/actuator is dragged), so the clutch gate is a modeling-scope decision, not a physics impossibility - but it is the decided scope. The statement is, however, imprecise as written: it omits the pedal_attachment=='spindle' conjunct that both the implemented config check (validate.cpp:1330) and request_crank_reposition (runtime.cpp:848-851) require, and it claims wire-boundary enforcement that does not exist.

## 4. Sufficiency Challenge (Admitted Invalid Scenarios)
Even the full intended guard leaves gaps: (1) reposition_on_stall=true with pedaling.enabled=false is admitted and still fires - the 'enabled' parameter in PedalingPolicy::update is the runtime rider-enable (drivetrain.cpp:679), while config_.enabled only gates the cadence-coast trigger (pedaling.cpp:136), so a 'disabled' pedaling block still backdrives cranks. (2) Restore/unwind bypasses config gates entirely: PedalingPolicy::set_state (pedaling.cpp:30-42) and the _reposition codec (drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:286-322) admit an engaged reposition snapshot regardless of reposition_on_stall or destination topology; drivetrain::validate(DriveSnapshot,...) (writers/drivetrain.cpp:25-52) checks snapshot clutch topology vs config.motor_clutch but never pedaling.reposition vs topology, so a manoeuvre recorded under clutch+spindle resumes on a clutch-free restore. (3) reposition_on_stall=true in coast/ideal_speed_control + spindle is admitted yet permanently dormant (effort_=false -> enabled=false -> early return pedaling.cpp:64-78) - an inert-but-admitted config. (4) Source-of-truth split: the config guard reads typed config.articulated.pedal_attachment while request_crank_reposition reads the rider_controller wire map (runtime.cpp:837-847) - a bootstrap/recording can diverge them. (5) NaN/Inf on the flag is not expressible (bool members; wire kBoolean schema.cpp:667-669 enforces bool type), and all ten reposition knobs are scalar()'d finite+bounded (validate.cpp:598-610, writer_config.hpp:962-971).

## 5. Proposed Contract Revision
Restate the invariant as the conjunction the manoeuvre's own entry point enforces: 'drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall == true requires articulated.pedal_attachment == "spindle" AND drive.motor_clutch == true, matching NativeRideRuntime::request_crank_reposition() (runtime.cpp:848-855) and ruling #44.' Implementation: (a) extend validate.cpp:1330-1331 to also fail on !config.drive.motor_clutch; (b) mirror the clutch half on the wire boundary - wire::validate_config can join drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall to drive.motor_clutch inside the drive section (schema.cpp:1236-1256), while the attachment half needs rider_contacts (schema.cpp:1257-1261) or stays documented as typed-layer-only since the wire sections are separable; (c) mirror it in decode_drive (writer_config.hpp:1108-1112) for the Value/dict decode twins; (d) decide and document the dormant cases: reject reposition_on_stall in non-effort drive modes and/or with pedaling.enabled=false, or pin them as explicitly inert; (e) guard restore: reject or deterministically disengage a _reposition snapshot whose goal is armed when the destination config lacks spindle or the clutch (set_state/drivetrain::validate DriveSnapshot).

# Stage 4 (`R2`) Adversarial Audit Report — `P01c`: `Transmission` Tendon-Range Boundary Coherence

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Adversarial Compliance Verdict (`R2`):** `holds`
- **Adversarial Justification Verdict (`R2`):** `relevant_engineering_contract`

## 1. Compliance Challenge
The equation is enforced by construction on every advancing path and I found no reachable violation under the stated assumptions. Ideal mode: stage_reset_into (native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:316-317), stage_prepare_into (:340-341) and the ratio-changing branch of stage_ratio_into (:377-385) all execute `update.range[1] = *update.state.boundary` verbatim immediately after producing the boundary; commit() then copies update.range into model_->tendon_range (:419-423) and swaps update.state into state_ (:433). Geometric mode: linearize_candidate computes `upper = engaged(update.state.boundary) - phi + dot(geometry_.jacobian, q)` (:194-197) and stores the SAME phi/jacobian/q into update.prepared (:202-205), so the post-commit identity `range[1] == boundary - prepared.phi + dot(prepared.J, prepared.q)` holds bitwise (element-wise copies at :441-444 preserve the doubles exactly; dot() on identical operands reproduces the same sum). Non-ratio-changing set_ratio early-returns before any boundary work (:368-369) and is correctly out of scope. NaN/Inf inputs are caught before publish (positive(ratio) :367, validation::derived in linearize :195, finite/finite_optional in validate_state :611-623, validated_rear_teeth :261-269); a throwing stage never reaches commit, so nothing publishes. Edge cases checked and all coherent: geometric set_ratio with disengaged boundary (old_gap=0, :392-395), prepared_valid_=false after a prepared-less restore (make_update_into :241-252 then linearize re-engages), writer-composed reset for both hub kinds (writers/drivetrain.cpp:290-316), and the writer's post-stage boundary override for the ideal reset (:303-313) which also rewrites update.range[1]. Residual scope-edge concerns, not equation violations: (1) Transmission::commit() is public and runs ZERO validation — restore() validates first (:643) but commit() trusts the update; the writer itself mutates staged candidates before commit (drivetrain.cpp:297 clears shift_pending, :303-313 overrides boundary+range), so the invariant survives only because every current mutation site also updates update.range[1] — nothing checks it at the commit boundary; (2) commit() reads update.coefficients[i] for i < coefficients_.size() (:410-414) — an undersized update.coefficients gives an out-of-bounds read and garbage wrap_prm; (3) stage_ratio_into writes `update.coefficients[0] = ratio` (:378) with no size guard — the public composed overload stage_ratio(data, ratio, base) (:359-363) with an unseeded TransmissionUpdate{} base is an OOB write on an empty vector in ideal mode (geometric incidentally throws later at validated_rear_teeth with front_teeth=0); (4) commit phase-1 writes wrap_prm before engine::set_const (:409-418) — a mid-commit engine failure leaves coefficients changed, tendon_range unchanged, state_ unpublished (the documented poison contract; mitigated because evaluate_candidate pre-flies the same call on scratch :287-300); (5) ideal prepare() swallows a NaN phi through std::min(B, NaN)=B (:338-339) and commits successfully where geometric throws in derived() — silent acceptance of corrupt input, though the equation itself still holds.

## 2. Concrete Counterexamples / Repros
No counterexample exists among advancing reset()/prepare()/ratio-changing set_ratio() under valid model state — the equation holds mechanically. Reachable coherence violations exist only on sibling paths the statement does not name: (A) Transmission::restore() publishes caller-supplied range/boundary/prepared with no cross-check — validate() (:603-640) verifies finiteness, widths, rear_teeth>=3 and the ideal coefficient/ratio coupling (:606) but never compares range[1] to boundary or prepared; repro exercised by the test suite itself: test_injected_valid_state_and_replay injects `boundary.value() += .02` while leaving `range` untouched (native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:335) and restore() commits it (:341) — afterwards model_->tendon_range[1] == old_bound while *state_.boundary == old_bound+0.02 until the next advancing call; (B) a forged or mutated TransmissionUpdate committed through public Transmission::commit() — `auto u = t.stage_prepare(d); u.state.boundary += 1.; t.commit(u);` publishes tendon_range[1] != boundary-... with no throw because commit() runs no validation at all (:404-447); (C) stage_ratio(data, ratio, TransmissionUpdate{}) through the public composed overload — update.coefficients[0] on an empty vector is UB in ideal mode (:378); (D) restore_equilibrium_state writes model_tendon_range and wrap_prm wholesale from a state file (native/src/initialization/equilibrium_cache.cpp:264-268) along with qpos (:270) AFTER drive().reset() established the boundary (initial_state.cpp:208; call sites equilibrium.cpp:100 and initial_state.cpp:190), leaving the file's bound and a pre-restore-pose boundary live while subsequent forward() calls run (equilibrium.cpp:103, initial_state.cpp:196); (E) a geometric restore with prepared absent is accepted (validated by test_geometric_prepared_sentinel_owns_reused_storage, test_native_drivetrain.cpp:583-585) and the next settle()/solved() then throws `prepare the geometric transmission before solving` (:509-510) — an admitted dead state until the next advancing tick.

## 3. Necessity Challenge (Blocked Valid Scenarios)
Nothing valid is blocked, and the statement's scope cut is correct. For advancing calls the bound MUST move with the boundary: the ideal freehub constraint is `ratio*q_crank - q_wheel <= range[1]` and during a shift cut the ratio sweeps continuously each tick (writers/drivetrain.cpp:724-740), so boundary and range[1] must stay glued or the sweep applies a physically wrong bound; the shift-energy bookkeeping requires it too — stage_ratio computes shift_parameter_work as last_tension*(new_gap-old_gap) where gap = boundary - phi (:391-398), and stage_solved's defect/work telemetry consume boundary and prepared (:517-518, :536-540); the geometric clause is literally the definition of the linearized limit (J.q <= range[1] <=> phi + J.(q - q_prep) <= boundary), not a redundant restatement. No product scenario needs an advancing call to publish range[1] != boundary-derived: ratchet semantics make boundary the single source of truth (reset re-baselines it :308/:315, prepare min-ratchets it :333/:338). Enforcing the equation on restore() WOULD wrongly block tested product behavior — the +0.02 boundary injection for continuation (test_native_drivetrain.cpp:335) and the absent-prepared sentinel (:583) — so the property is correctly scoped to advancing calls, but the transient post-restore incoherence is contractual, not accidental.

## 4. Sufficiency Challenge (Admitted Invalid Scenarios)
Even with the statement holding on every advancing call, the following invalid states are admitted: (1) restore() publishes ANY finite range[1]/boundary/prepared triple — state_codec decodes the three independently (drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:355-374), Transmission::restore copies s.range verbatim into the model (:651, :655-656) and validate() never checks the equation; writer-level validate() (writers/drivetrain.cpp:73-81) checks rear_teeth, coefficient width and coefficients==prepared->jacobian — every sibling coherence EXCEPT range<->boundary — making the omission conspicuous; (2) restored prepared carries arbitrary finite phi/jacobian/qpos (validate_state :628-639 checks only width and finiteness), so stage_solved's defect/work bookkeeping (:513-518, :522-523) silently consumes a physically meaningless linearization reference; (3) boundary=null restores (optional_num codec :355, finite_optional admits disengaged :614, prepared-without-boundary is the only rejected combination :630-632 and test_prepared_state_requires_boundary) leave a live physical bound with no logical boundary; (4) forged/mutated TransmissionUpdates pass through public commit() unvalidated — including OOB reads when update.coefficients.size() < coefficients_.size() (:410-414); (5) external model mutation is laundered into logical state: make_update_into seeds update.range/update.coefficients from LIVE wrap_prm/tendon_range (:227-234) while update.state is logical, and a no-change set_ratio early-returns BEFORE evaluate_candidate (:368-369), so an externally-mutated wrap_prm commits a snapshot whose state_.coefficients[0] != state_.ratio — the exact invariant validate() itself enforces on restore (:606); the code's own comment accepts external mutation as 'truthful, not a contract violation' (:272-278); (6) commit() phase-1 partial writes (wrap_prm committed, then set_const failure) leave the model inconsistent with the unpublished state_ under the E1 poison contract; (7) ideal prepare() silently commits on NaN qpos (std::min swallows NaN, :338-339) where geometric prepare throws; (8) tendon_range/wrap_prm are writable outside Transmission — restore_equilibrium_state (equilibrium_cache.cpp:265), the declared mutable model arrays for frames (runtime/presentation.cpp:29), and the GPU batch twin that writes tendon_range[1] itself as tendon_upper_bound_rad (gpu/batch_step.hpp:213-218) — none of these is visible to the file-scoped property; (9) state() mirrors range LIVE from the model (:588-590) while boundary is logical, so post-restore/pre-advance snapshots report — and re-restoring persists — the incoherence; (10) a staged update committed after an intervening physics step stays self-consistent (prepared.qpos is the stale frame too) but the bound is physically retargeted — the writer guards this with commit_epoch_ (drivetrain.cpp:581-586), Transmission::commit itself does not.

## 5. Proposed Contract Revision
Restate as a commit-granularity postcondition with explicit exclusions and enforce it at the two unguarded gates: (1) 'Every committed TransmissionUpdate (via reset(), prepare(), ratio-changing set_ratio(), or stage_* -> commit) leaves model_->tendon_range[2*tendon+1] == *state_.boundary (ideal) or *state_.boundary - prepared.phi + dot(prepared.jacobian, prepared.qpos) (geometric, prepared engaged)' — this catches the forged/mutated-update hole the current per-call formulation misses, since Transmission::commit() is public and validates nothing while the writer demonstrates post-stage mutation is an accepted pattern (drivetrain.cpp:297,303-313). (2) Add the equation to validate()/validate_state() alongside the existing sibling checks: ideal `!s.boundary || s.range[1] == *s.boundary`; geometric `!s.prepared || s.range[1] == *s.boundary - s.prepared->phi + dot(...)` — but apply it at commit() (cheap, once per publish) NOT unconditionally at restore(), because the tested injection workflow (test_native_drivetrain.cpp:335) and the absent-prepared sentinel (:583) require restore to admit incoherent pairs; if a product decision later demands post-restore coherence, that is a restore-contract change, not a P01c change. (3) Guard stage_ratio_into's `update.coefficients[0]` write and commit()'s coefficient loop with an explicit `update.coefficients.size() == coefficients_.size()` check (:378, :410) so an unseeded composed base fails loudly instead of hitting UB. (4) Document the explicit exclusions: restore(), restore_equilibrium_state's model-row writes (equilibrium_cache.cpp:265), presentation-frame model arrays, the GPU batch bound writer (batch_step.hpp:213-218), and external row mutation — the invariant holds only until the next non-transmission writer; if a system-level 'the bound always equals the boundary' guarantee is intended, it must include those paths or be restated as 'at every advancing publication'. (5) Optionally align ideal prepare()'s NaN handling with geometric (finite(phi) before the min) so corrupt input rejects instead of silently republishing the old boundary.

# Stage 4 (`R2`) Adversarial Audit Report — `P02a`: Cross-Boundary State-Space Coherence (Runtime, `set_state`, Wire Codec)

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Adversarial Compliance Verdict (`R2`):** `partially_violated`
- **Adversarial Justification Verdict (`R2`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Compliance Challenge
The statement claims each clause holds 'in both decode_policies and PedalingPolicy::set_state'. Verified field-by-field: (a') is enforced at NEITHER boundary — decode_policies reads crank_rad/wheel_rad via bare `num` (state_codec.cpp:294-295 -> ncd::num = finite_real only, writer_config.hpp:288-291) with no zero-check when stall_elapsed_s is null (line 296 optional_num), and set_state runs only `finite` on both (pedaling.cpp:32-33). The parenthetical mechanism claim is also wrong: stall_update does NOT zero the accumulators when arming — arming (stall_detector.hpp:22 `!s.elapsed_s -> s.elapsed_s=0; return`) preserves them; only the `s={}` resets on !loaded/progress-exceeded/window-fired (lines 21,26,29) zero them, and every path that leaves elapsed_s null passes through `s={}`. So (a') holds as an emergent invariant of update()-produced states, not because arming sanitizes. (b') is enforced at NEITHER boundary: decode admits any {goal_rad,reason,cooldown_s} combination with only reason clamped to {0,1,2} (state_codec.cpp:297-312); set_state checks only finiteness/nonnegativity/reason<=2 (pedaling.cpp:34-41). All three subclauses — goal<->reason biconditional, goal-vs-cooldown exclusion, goal=>(!coasting && effort==0) — have concrete admitted counterexamples that flow into live objects. (c') is enforced ONLY at set_state (coasting=>target_phase pedaling.cpp:26; dec>=0 line 24; effort>=0 line 25) and is wholly absent from decode_policies: coasting:true with target_phase_rad:null, deceleration_rad_s2:-1, _effort:-5 all decode successfully (state_codec.cpp:315-322). Downstream gates do not rescue (a')/(b'): DrivetrainWriter::restore (drivetrain.cpp:1213-1261) runs validate() which checks only transmission topology, pending-actuation clock, shift count/direction, cassette and ratio relations (drivetrain.cpp:25-85) then calls set_state at :1218; PolicySet::restore (policy_set.hpp:34-41) adds only shift/cassette checks (validate_state, :46-57). Reachable admission chain: recording/bootstrap `state_native_drive` doc -> decode_drive_snapshot (bootstrap.cpp:376; initial_state.cpp:139; equilibrium_cache.cpp:83) -> drive().restore (runtime.cpp:527; equilibrium_cache.cpp:155) -> live pedaling_. NaN/Inf ARE correctly rejected at both boundaries (VNode::real admits the $nonfinite tag but finite_real re-rejects, state_codec.cpp:98-131; set_state finite/finite_optional/nonnegative, validation.hpp:10-29). Net: every relational clause fails 'in both'; only the scalar domains and (c')-at-set_state are real.

## 2. Concrete Counterexamples / Repros
All repros ride a `state.drive` document whose policies.pedaling._reposition member is hand-authored; minimal valid skeleton = the fixture mutated per test_state_codecs.cpp:330-339 (with_reposition). REPRO A (violates (a') at both boundaries): _reposition={"crank_rad":5.0,"wheel_rad":-2.0,"stall_elapsed_s":null,"goal_rad":null,"elapsed_s":0.0,"cooldown_s":0.0,"armed":true,"reason":0} — decode_drive_snapshot accepts (state_codec.cpp:294-312); p.set_state accepts (pedaling.cpp:32-41); restore publishes it live. Consequence: first loaded update() arms the detector (stall_detector.hpp:22) keeping crank_rad=5 (copied back at pedaling.cpp:93,99); next tick 5+rate*dt > reposition_stall_progress_rad (=0.5) trips the progress reset (stall_detector.hpp:25-26) — phantom 5-rad progress accumulated while disarmed corrupts the stall window. REPRO B (violates each (b') subclause at both boundaries): B1 {"goal_rad":1.0,"reason":0,...} — admitted; update() executes the manoeuvre (pedaling.cpp:110-122) and misreports .reason as reposition_stall since line 119 maps any reason!=1 to the stall label — telemetry misattributes a reason-0 manoeuvre. B2 {"goal_rad":null,"reason":2,...} — admitted dead residue breaking the biconditional. B3 {"goal_rad":1.0,"cooldown_s":5.0,"reason":1} — admitted; cooldown ticks (line 87) while the goal manoeuvre runs — an automaton-impossible combination (runtime only ever sets cooldown when clearing goal, lines 88,114). B4 pedaling={"coasting":true,"target_phase_rad":0.5,"_effort":42.0,...} + _reposition={"goal_rad":1.0,"reason":1,...} — passes BOTH boundaries (effort>=0 ok, coasting has target, goal finite); then update() early-returns the reposition result (pedaling.cpp:110-122) WITHOUT touching state_.coasting/state_.effort (only the arm path at :106-107 clears them), so every emitted snapshot during the manoeuvre keeps violating goal=>(!coasting && effort==0) — admitted corruption propagates instead of self-healing. REPRO C (violates (c') at decode_policies only): pedaling={"coasting":true,"target_phase_rad":null,"target_rate_rad_s":2.0,"deceleration_rad_s2":-3.0,"_effort":-1.0,"_cadence_ema":null} — decode_drive_snapshot/decode_policy_snapshot SUCCEED and return the incoherent typed snapshot (state_codec.cpp:315-322 has no coasting/target or sign checks); only restore->set_state rejects it (pedaling.cpp:24-26). Codec-only consumers (decode_policy_snapshot callers, replay/inspection paths such as policy_reference_support.hpp:58-59) therefore observe states violating (c').

## 3. Necessity Challenge (Blocked Valid Scenarios)
As a codec-admission rule the statement conflicts with confirmed boundary design and a pinned test: (1) checks_drive_reposition requires the exact combination {crank_rad:.4, wheel_rad:-1.25, stall_elapsed_s:.62, goal_rad:1.0, cooldown_s:.25, reason:2} — violating `!(goal && cooldown>0)` — to round-trip field-exact (test_state_codecs.cpp:285-296), and then requires {stall_elapsed_s:null, goal_rad:null} WITH the same nonzero accumulators — directly violating (a') — to round-trip too (:319-325); enforcing either clause inside decode_policies breaks this committed test. (2) The codec header pins decode(encode(x)) == x on well-formed snapshots (state_codec.hpp:20) and the file documents 'staging-only by construction' (state_codec.cpp:15-16): the wire boundary is deliberately per-lane domain validation; relational coherence is restore()/set_state's job — enforcing (a')/(b')/(c') in the codec would make transported-but-incoherent states non-representable, contradicting the two-layer contract. (3) The implementation plan explicitly decided thinness: 'set_state gets no new checks beyond finite_optional(s.reposition->goal_rad, ...) when engaged' (docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-03-crank-reposition-native.md:563). As a LIVE-STATE invariant the clauses block nothing valid: every runtime production path satisfies them — goal is armed only with reason in {1,2}, effort=0, coasting=false (pedaling.cpp:106-107) and the early return at :118-122 preserves that; cooldown>0 co-occurs with goal never (set only at :88,:114 which also clear goal; edge guard :101); stall_elapsed_s becomes null only via `s={}` which zeroes both accumulators (stall_detector.hpp:21,26,29); state_.coasting is set only where target_phase_rad/deceleration were just written (:199-205) and cleared by reset() (:146). Legitimate residue (elapsed_s>0 with goal cleared — elapsed is intentionally NOT reset at :113-114; crank/wheel residue is cleared whenever loaded=false) is already compatible with the stated clauses, so the property was calibrated around real residue patterns and blocks no runtime-produced snapshot. Verdict on necessity: wrong boundary for the codec clauses (contrary to pinned staging design); sound as a postcondition on emitted/live state; implementable at set_state at zero product cost.

## 4. Sufficiency Challenge (Admitted Invalid Scenarios)
Even if every stated clause were enforced, these corrupt states remain admissible at the same boundaries: (1) Missing converse of (c'): coasting:false + engaged target_phase_rad/deceleration_rad_s2/target_rate_rad_s — runtime never produces it (target is written only in the coasting branch, pedaling.cpp:199-204, and reset() clears at :146); admitted by codec and set_state (one-directional check at :26). (2) _cadence_ema sign unchecked: optional_num/finite_optional only (state_codec.cpp:321, pedaling.cpp:22) while update() assigns actual=|rate|*60/2pi >= 0 (:130-134); a restored _cadence_ema=-1e9 makes `excessive` (cadence_ema >= threshold, :136) permanently false — suppresses cadence-triggered coasting for the session; semantic corruption no clause covers. (3) Unbounded magnitudes: _effort has only >=0 (pedaling.cpp:25) — _effort=1e9 admitted, and with effort_slew_nm_s>0 the slew clamp (max(prev-delta, min(prev+delta,target)), :164-170) bleeds it down by slew*dt per tick so ~1e9 N*m of 'human' torque persists effectively forever (runtime bounds effort by input/ceiling/mash). cooldown_s/elapsed_s/deceleration_rad_s2/target_rate_rad_s/target_phase_rad are likewise nonneg-or-finite only (state_codec.cpp:298-305, pedaling.cpp:23-24,37-39): cooldown_s=1e9 admitted suppresses all future manoeuvres ~indefinitely; coasting target_rate=1e9 persists as the spin target. (4) No config-vs-state coherence: a seeded goal_rad executes the manoeuvre regardless of config_.reposition_on_stall (pedaling.cpp:110 never consults it; only the detector's `loaded` at :91 does) — unlike transmission topology/cassette which ARE config-checked in validate() (drivetrain.cpp:30-32,44-52,73-84); an engaged _reposition under a clutch-free motor_clutch=false topology is admitted although request_crank_reposition is rejected at runtime for exactly that topology (runtime.cpp:830+; RIDE.md:188). (5) Snapshot-vs-model incoherence admitted: goal_rad arbitrarily far from the live crank qpos (self-heals via :111-114 only if remaining<=tolerance or timeout), stall_elapsed_s>=window residue, accumulators already beyond progress threshold while armed — all pass every stated clause. (6) armed flag unconstrained: armed=false persists only while a request is held in runtime (:90) but an admitted armed=false endures indefinitely unless reposition machinery is exercised (disengage at :124 requires armed). The deeper gap: reposition/pedaling lacks the relational gate that shifting/transmission already have in validate() — the stated clauses cover only three couplings of an eight-field sub-object.

## 5. Proposed Contract Revision
Split the property into two statements matching the confirmed layering. (I) RUNTIME INVARIANT (postcondition over snapshots emitted by PedalingPolicy::update / committed ticks / DrivetrainWriter::state()): keep all of (a'),(b'),(c') — they hold for update()-produced states — but fix the (a') mechanism note: accumulators are zeroed by the `s={}` resets on !loaded/progress/window (stall_detector.hpp:21,26,29), while arming (:22) preserves them; (a') holds because elapsed_s=null is reachable only via `s={}`. Optionally strengthen with the true converses runtime also maintains: target_phase_rad.has_value() <=> s.coasting (not just =>), and `stall_elapsed_s.has_value() => detector armed` (accumulators may be nonzero — wheel_rad legitimately negative under rollback, :23-24). (II) ADMISSION CONTRACT (per boundary): decode_policies guarantees ONLY per-lane domains — closed key sets (state_codec.cpp:289-292), finite reals, nonneg time lanes (:302-308), reason in {0,1,2} (:309-312) — it must NOT enforce relational clauses, per the pinned round-trip test (test_state_codecs.cpp:285-325), the decode∘encode identity (state_codec.hpp:20) and the staging-only design (:15-16). set_state currently guarantees scalar domains + (c') only (pedaling.cpp:21-41); if relational hardening is desired it belongs HERE or in validate() — never in the codec — and should add: the (a') accumulator/disarm coherence, all three (b') couplings, the target_phase=>coasting converse, _cadence_ema>=0, and magnitude ceilings for _effort/cooldown_s/elapsed_s; optionally a config-vs-state check (engaged reposition under reposition_on_stall=false / motor_clutch=false) mirroring the transmission-topology gate (drivetrain.cpp:30-52). No runtime-produced snapshot is blocked by any of these set_state additions since update() satisfies them all.

# Stage 4 (`R2`) Adversarial Audit Report — `P02b`: Bounded Crank-Reposition Termination & Post-State

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Adversarial Compliance Verdict (`R2`):** `partially_violated`
- **Adversarial Justification Verdict (`R2`):** `relevant_engineering_contract`

## 1. Compliance Challenge
Core mechanism verified at native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:82-124: goal_rad is disengaged by braking (line 88) or when `remaining <= reposition_phase_tolerance_rad || r.elapsed_s > reposition_timeout_s` (lines 111-114); both exits set reason=0 and cooldown_s=reposition_cooldown_s, and the object tears down iff `!reposition_on_stall && !goal && cooldown_s<=0 && armed` (line 124). The resume slew clamps effort to previous ± effort_slew_nm_s*dt (lines 164-169). However the stated termination bound is mis-stated under the natural reading of 'cumulative active-manoeuvre steps': r.elapsed_s += dt runs BEFORE the timeout check (line 112 precedes line 113), so the accumulated elapsed_s at the disengaging step lies in (timeout_s, timeout_s + dt], not <= timeout_s + 0.5*ulp(timeout_s). The 0.5-ulp term confuses ulp-granularity strictness with dt-granularity sampling: the overshoot is up to one full step, and is exactly timeout_s + dt when a prefix sum lands on timeout_s (strict `>` does not fire at equality). Second hole: the assumption 'dt >= dt_min > 0 (no binary64 absorption)' does not imply its parenthetical. Absorption depends on dt vs ulp(elapsed_s): elapsed_s += dt freezes once elapsed ~ dt*2^53. For timeout=3.0 any dt < ~3.3e-16 (finite, positive, passes positive(dt) at line 61) lets elapsed stall below the timeout; with a frozen crank and no braking the manoeuvre NEVER terminates — liveness fails under the letter of the assumptions; the dt floor must be quantified (dt > ulp(timeout_s)/2 throughout the accumulation). Third: 'effort ramps from previous == 0' holds only because line 107 zeroes state_.effort on the edge path; set_state (lines 20-44) and the wire codec (state_codec.cpp:287-313) admit goal_rad engaged with _effort > 0, so a codec-valid restore reaches the pedaling transition with previous != 0 and the claimed ramp-from-zero fails.

## 2. Concrete Counterexamples / Repros
R1 (bound overshoot, reachable): config timeout_s=1.0, phase_tolerance=0.05, noop=0.12, cooldown=1.0, on_stall=true, min_effort=20, slew>0; calls with phase held at kDeadSpot (phase mod pi = pi/2), rate=0, required=0, effort=20, dt=0.5, enabled=true, braking=false. Step 0 (reposition_request=true): turn=pi/2>noop => goal=phase-pi/2, elapsed=0.5, emits reposition. Step 1: elapsed=1.0, 1.0>1.0 false => still active. Step 2: elapsed=1.5>1.0 => disengages at cumulative active duration 1.5 >> bound ~= 1.0+1.1e-16. R2 (extreme): timeout=0.4, dt=10.0 => the first step after goal-set disengages at elapsed=10, 25x the stated bound. R3 (non-termination): timeout=3.0, dt=1e-16, frozen phase, effort>=min_effort, no braking: ulp(3.0)/2 ~= 2.2e-16 > dt means elapsed_s stalls once elapsed >= dt*2^53 ~= 0.9; timeout is never crossed and goal_rad never disengages — violates bounded liveness under the stated assumptions (dt_min unquantified). R4 (restore breaks 'previous==0'): set_state({reposition:{goal_rad:g finite, reason:1, elapsed_s:0, cooldown_s:0, armed:true}, effort:50, coasting:false}) passes every gate at pedaling.cpp:20-44 (nonnegative(s.effort) but no goal<=>effort==0 link); the same document decodes cleanly at state_codec.cpp:287-313. Next update with effort=20, dt: manoeuvre clears on timeout/tolerance, lands in pedaling, and effort = clamp(target, 50 +/- slew*dt) — e.g. slew=5, dt=0.001, target=30 yields effort=49.995, ramping DOWN from the corrupt 50, not up from 0. R5 (phantom set+clear): config noop=0.02, tolerance=0.5 (validate() at config_validation.hpp:138-141 does not order the two knobs); request at turn=0.1: goal set at line 106 then remaining=0.1<=0.5 clears it in the SAME call — zero emitted manoeuvre steps, request edge consumed, cooldown charged. Shipped profiles (noop=0.12 > tol=0.05) avoid this, but the admitted config domain does not.

## 3. Necessity Challenge (Blocked Valid Scenarios)
Under the deadline reading, the stated bound condemns the product's own tested timeout-exit scenario: test_pedaling_policy.cpp:163-177 drives a frozen dead-spot crank to the timeout and accepts ticks*kDt <= 3.5 for timeout=3.0 — the implementation legitimately keeps the goal engaged past timeout+ulp (elapsed at clear ~= 3.00125). So the bound clause, as written, blocks a scenario the project test suite pins as correct; it needs the +dt term (or an emitting-steps semantics, under which cumulative emitted duration <= timeout and the ulp fudge is unnecessary). All other clauses are confirmed by product artifacts: braking abort + cooldown (test_pedaling_policy.cpp:121-130), tolerance exit + cooldown + slew-from-zero resume (lines 136-161: `s.effort_nm <= 300.*kDt`), cooldown-consumed press and post-cooldown re-arm (lines 178-205), object teardown after cooldown when !on_stall (lines 207-224), and RIDE.md:166-186 / simulation-decisions.md:490-519 (decision 44) documenting the bounded manoeuvre, zero delivered effort during it, and the resume semantics. No valid scenario is wrongly blocked by the post-state, teardown, or slew clauses.

## 4. Sufficiency Challenge (Admitted Invalid Scenarios)
Even where the statement holds, the restore/commit admission path accepts live-unreachable states whose effects the contract does not cover: (a) goal_rad engaged with _effort>0 — codec state_codec.cpp:287-313 checks only per-field domains (reason in {0,1,2}, elapsed_s/cooldown_s/stall_elapsed_s >= 0); set_state pedaling.cpp:20-44 adds finite/nonnegative checks but no cross-field invariant — the ramp-from-zero premise is silently violated (repro R4). (b) goal_rad engaged with reason=0 — admitted; line 119 maps reason!=1 to reposition_stall, so an 'idle'-reasoned goal emits the stall telemetry label. (c) goal engaged with coasting=true (target_phase_rad set, as required by line 26) — admitted; on disengage-to-coast, `if (!state_.coasting)` at line 199 is false, so a STALE target_phase/target_rate/deceleration trajectory resumes instead of a fresh |rate|/stop_time_s capture. (d) coasting=true with deceleration_rad_s2=0 and target_rate!=0 — admitted (deceleration only nonnegative); the coasting FSM then never decays the rate (lines 206-208), an eternal fixed-rate coast reachable by restore but not by live paths. (e) config domain: reposition_noop_rad < reposition_phase_tolerance_rad admitted (repro R5) produces set+same-step-clear phantom manoeuvres. (f) benign self-healing admissions: stall_elapsed_s engaged while !reposition_on_stall, armed=false on an idle object (re-armed next non-request step, line 90), elapsed_s>timeout with no goal. Also noted: validation::derived can throw mid-update (lines 158/160) after the reset()+partial-restore at lines 143-149 — a half-transitioned state survives an overflow throw, unlike the atomic top-of-update rejections the invalid_calls_preserve_state fixtures pin.

## 5. Proposed Contract Revision
Restate the bound in step-quantized terms: 'r.goal_rad disengages on the first active-manoeuvre update at which accumulated r.elapsed_s strictly exceeds reposition_timeout_s — i.e., the accumulated active duration at disengagement lies in (reposition_timeout_s, reposition_timeout_s + dt] (one-step overshoot; the upper endpoint is reached when a prefix sum equals the timeout exactly, since the test is strict `>`) — or earlier on braking or phase - *goal_rad <= reposition_phase_tolerance_rad, provided dt stays above the binary64 absorption threshold over the whole accumulation (strengthen the assumption to e.g. dt > ulp(reposition_timeout_s)/2, not merely dt_min > 0). On disengagement reason=0 and cooldown_s = reposition_cooldown_s; state_.reposition disengages at line 124 iff !reposition_on_stall && cooldown_s<=0 && armed (equivalently reposition_cooldown_s==0 on the disengage step). On the subsequent PedalMode::pedaling transition effort = clamp(target, previous +/- effort_slew_nm_s*dt) for slew>0, = target for slew==0, where previous = state_.effort — 0 only when the goal was set via the edge path (line 107); a valid initial state must therefore state explicitly that goal_rad engaged implies state_.effort == 0, reason in {1,2}, and coasting == false, or set_state must enforce those implications to keep the contract total over codec-admissible snapshots.'

# Stage 4 (`R2`) Adversarial Audit Report — `P03a`: Pedaling Effort Slew & Coasting Zero-Effort Contract

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Adversarial Compliance Verdict (`R2`):** `partially_violated`
- **Adversarial Justification Verdict (`R2`):** `relevant_confirmed_requirement`

## 1. Compliance Challenge
Clause-by-clause. (1) Slew bound: the mechanism at native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:164-169 clamps effort to [previous-delta, previous+delta] with previous = state_.effort (line 143) and delta = effort_slew_nm_s*dt, so the bound holds iff state_.effort still equals the effort the last update published. Two reachable paths break that premise. (a) The reposition edge at pedaling.cpp:106-108 writes state_.effort = 0. unconditionally at goal creation, while the goal can be cleared on the SAME tick at :113-114 whenever remaining = turn <= config_.reposition_phase_tolerance_rad. Since validate() bounds the two knobs independently (validate.cpp:600 noop in [0, pi/2), :604 tolerance merely > 0, no cross-relation), any config with reposition_phase_tolerance_rad > reposition_noop_rad admits turn in (noop, min(tolerance, pi-noop)] -> a goal is created, zeroes the stored effort, and dies instantly; execution falls through to the pedaling path with previous = 0 and returns effort_k = min(slew*dt, target). The previous tick's published effort (e.g. 50 N*m) drops to ~0.3 in one update: |delta| = 49.7 >> slew*dt = 0.3, violating clause 1 on both the returned effort_nm sequence and the stored state_.effort sequence. This is a faithful port of the Python oracle (c809f12a^ pedaling.py: self._effort = 0. at goal creation plus the same-tick done check), i.e. a latent upstream edge, not a port regression; shipped profiles are safe only because tolerance (0.05) < noop (0.12) there. (b) Via set_state injection (clause 3 is unimplemented): a restored reposition.goal_rad + effort != 0 survives the reposition early-return at :118-121, which never re-zeroes state_.effort, so every manoeuvre tick reports effort_nm = 0 while the stored baseline stays injected; when the goal clears, previous = injected value and the returned effort jumps |injected - 0| >> delta. A restored coasting=true + effort != 0 similarly hands the next pedaling tick a phantom previous at :143. Other edges checked and clean: slew*dt overflow throws via derived() (validation.hpp:31-34); slew <= 0 is deliberately unbounded and correctly scoped out of the statement; mash/ceiling/torque_factor only move target inside the clamp; disabled/coasting/pedaling-entry all zero or preserve the baseline consistently. (2) Zero-effort: all three non-pedaling returns hardcode effort_nm = 0. (pedaling.cpp:74 disabled, :120 reposition-coasting, :212 coasting) - clause 2 holds exactly on the returned value; only the stored field can diverge via the unguarded set_state path in (1b). (3) set_state rejection: pedaling.cpp:20-44 validates finiteness/nonnegativity, the coasting->target_phase_rad implication (:26) and the reposition sub-object (:30-42), but contains NO effort-vs-mode check: set_state({.coasting=true, .target_phase_rad=0., .effort=50.}) and set_state({.reposition={.goal_rad=1.}, .effort=50.}) are both admitted. Reachable in production: DrivetrainWriter::restore (writers/drivetrain.cpp:1213-1220) routes decoded snapshots through validate() (drivetrain.cpp:25-85, no pedaling effort checks) into p.set_state; the wire codec decode_policies (drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:268-339) likewise enforces no coasting/goal->effort==0 relation, and real() (:98-124) even passes $nonfinite tags through to be caught later by set_state's finite/nonnegative gates - confirming set_state is the sole semantic gate and the missing checks are a real hole, not layered redundancy.

## 2. Concrete Counterexamples / Repros
REPRO A (clause 1, internal, admitted-config): PedalingConfig c = shipped defaults (coast_above_rpm=110, resume_below_rpm=90, stop_time_s=0.35, mash_cadence_rpm=45, mash_torque_nm=60, effort_slew_nm_s=300, reposition_cooldown_s=1, reposition_timeout_s=3, reposition_back_rate_rad_s=3, reposition_noop_rad=0.12, reposition_on_stall=false) except reposition_phase_tolerance_rad = 1.0 (passes post_init: scalar > 0, validate.cpp:604; ctor accepts). Sequence: (i) p.update(phase=0.6, rate=8.0, required=0, effort=50, dt=0.5, enabled=true, braking=false, reposition_request=false) -> reason none, previous=0, delta=150 -> returns {pedaling, effort_nm=50}, state_.effort=50 (bound held: |50-0|=50<=150). (ii) p.update(phase=0.5, rate=8.0, required=0, effort=50, dt=0.001, enabled=true, braking=false, reposition_request=true): tracked -> emplace reposition (armed=true, config_types.hpp:71); cooldown=0; request_edge=true (pedaling.cpp:90); loaded=false so reflex_edge=false (stall_detector.hpp:21); edge loop -> rel=0.5, turn=fmod(0.5,pi)=0.5, min(0.5,2.64)=0.5>noop -> goal_rad=0.0, state_.effort=0. (:106-107); then remaining=0.5 <= tolerance 1.0 -> goal cleared, cooldown=1. (:112-114); falls through: required=0, actual=76.4 rpm < 110, reason=none (:137-141) -> pedaling path: previous = state_.effort = 0 -> effort = clamp(50, -0.3, 0.3) = 0.3 -> returns {pedaling, effort_nm=0.3}. Published effort sequence 50 -> 0.3 with |delta|=49.7 > slew*dt=0.3: VIOLATION. With tolerance >= pi - noop (e.g. 3.1, still admitted) EVERY non-noop phase triggers the same wipe, degenerating the whole manoeuvre into a silent effort reset while returning pedaling. REPRO B (clause 3, direct): PedalingPolicy p(valid_config); p.set_state({.coasting=true, .target_phase_rad=0., .target_rate_rad_s=5., .deceleration_rad_s2=1., .effort=50.}) - all checks at pedaling.cpp:21-42 pass, state_ published. Then p.update(phase=0, rate=8, required=0, effort=50, dt=0.001) -> reason none -> previous=50 -> returns effort_nm ~50, a phantom baseline the slew bound never saw develop (and had the previous published effort been 0, |50-0|>0.3). REPRO B2: p.set_state({.reposition=RepositionSnapshot{.goal_rad=1.0,.elapsed_s=0.,.cooldown_s=0.,.reason=1}, .effort=50.}) admitted; updates at phase=3.0 keep returning coasting/reposition effort_nm=0 while state_.effort stays 50 (:118-121 never clears it); when phase descends to <=1.05 the goal clears at :113 and the same-tick pedaling return jumps to ~50: |50-0|=50 > 0.3. REPRO C (sufficiency): p.set_state({.coasting=true,.target_phase_rad=0.,.target_rate_rad_s=1e9,.deceleration_rad_s2=0.}) admitted; every coasting continuation computes next = max(0, |1e9| - 0*dt) = 1e9 (:207) - an undecaying phantom target rate integrated into target_phase forever; internally deceleration==0 only ever pairs with rate==0 (:200).

## 3. Necessity Challenge (Blocked Valid Scenarios)
None found; every subclause tracks a confirmed product invariant. Clause 1's slew is an explicit user decision (docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:198-202: shift-cut effort drop AND return run <= effort_slew_nm_s = 300 N*m/s 'not as a per-step jump', grounded in measured sprinter force development 560-870 N*m/s; an unclamped 13400 N*m/s step was rejected as nonphysical). The bound deliberately covers ceiling drops too (native/tests/reference/test_seated_pedaling_cycle.cpp:225-284: 'a 60 -> 0 ceiling step ramps down'), and slew <= 0 as an explicit 'off' switch (test_contracts.cpp:179-188) is correctly excluded by the statement's own 'for effort_slew_nm_s > 0' scoping. Clause 2 matches documented behaviour: RIDE.md:179-180 states the manoeuvre/coasting 'delivered pedal effort goes to 0 N*m', and simulation-decisions.md:501-503 fixes coasting-with-reposition-reason semantics; motor hold-over is a separate channel (AssistSnapshot.hold_*) so no scenario needs residual rider effort while coasting. Clause 3's predicate is exactly the invariant update() already maintains internally: state_.effort is forced to 0 at goal creation (pedaling.cpp:107), on every coasting tick (:210) and via reset() on disable (:65), so no update()-produced or encode()-produced snapshot can ever carry coasting|goal_rad with effort != 0; the commit path (drivetrain.cpp:1013, staged-validated at :654) would therefore never trip the rejection, and no legitimate wire document or restore is blocked. The only formal edge - rejection of a denormal/subnormal effort under exact `!= 0.` - is unreachable because internal code writes literal 0., never a subnormal.

## 4. Sufficiency Challenge (Admitted Invalid Scenarios)
Even with clause 3 implemented verbatim, these corrupt states remain admitted (all via set_state/restore; update() never produces them): (a) coasting=true, target_rate_rad_s != 0, deceleration_rad_s2 = 0 -> the coast servo never decays (pedaling.cpp:207 max(0,|prev|-0*dt)=|prev|): a permanent phantom spin target that integrates target_phase indefinitely; internally decel=0 only arises with rate=0. (b) coasting=true AND reposition.goal_rad both set: internally unreachable (goal creation forces coasting=false at :107), admitted under the stated predicate whenever effort==0; the stale flag then silently switches threshold selection to resume_below_rpm (:135) on the first post-goal tick. (c) cadence_ema = 1e9 with coast_cadence_tau_s > 0: finite_optional passes it (:22), then `excessive` (:136) stays true for ~coast_cadence_tau_s*ln(1e9/coast_above) seconds - an injected phantom cadence-coast that locks pedaling out; internally the EMA is bounded by observed cadence (:130-134). (d) effort = 1e9 with coasting=false and no goal: admitted (and only technically reachable since the effort input itself is unbounded); the slew then parks stored effort at ~1e9 decaying at 300 N*m/s - letter of the bound kept, physics corrupted. (e) reposition.stall_elapsed_s >= reposition_stall_window_s with reposition_on_stall=true: on the next loaded tick the detector fires a phantom reflex edge (:91-98) - one spurious manoeuvre. (f) reposition.reason in {1,2} with goal_rad absent: reason is set and cleared with the goal internally (:106,:88,:114); admitted as a benign residue (overwritten at the next goal). (g) reposition.elapsed_s arbitrarily large: admitted; times the goal out on the next tick - self-consistent but unreachable in shape. (h) Codec asymmetry: decode_policies (state_codec.cpp:315-322) performs no finite/nonnegative checks on target_rate_rad_s/deceleration_rad_s2/_effort and real() (:98-124) forwards $nonfinite tags, so decode_policy_snapshot alone yields objects that are NOT guaranteed set_state-admissible; rejection still happens at restore via set_state but reports internal names ('_effort', 'coasting target_phase_rad') instead of wire paths - diagnostic, not a safety hole. Separately, dt > reposition_timeout_s gives a same-tick create+clear too, but it is benign for clause 1 (the bound scales with dt) while still silently skipping the manoeuvre - a product-semantics note outside P03a's letter.

## 5. Proposed Contract Revision
Keep all three subclauses but close the demonstrated gaps. (A) Implement clause 3 exactly as stated in PedalingPolicy::set_state (pedaling.cpp:20-44): reject `(s.coasting || (s.reposition && s.reposition->goal_rad)) && s.effort != 0.`; additionally reject the internally-unreachable pair `s.coasting && s.reposition && s.reposition->goal_rad` (goal creation forces coasting=false at :107). (B) Close the internal clause-1 hole that clause 3 cannot reach: enforce `reposition_phase_tolerance_rad <= reposition_noop_rad` (and an upper bound `< pi/2` for sanity) in post_init(PedalingConfig) after validate.cpp:607 - then `turn > noop >= tolerance` guarantees a created goal can never satisfy `remaining <= tolerance` on its creation tick, eliminating same-tick create+clear structurally. As defence in depth, also move `state_.effort = 0.` from goal creation (:106-108) into the branch that actually publishes the reposition-coasting return (:118-121), so a goal that dies in its birth tick cannot wipe the slew baseline; deferring the zeroing changes no published behaviour on the manoeuvre path. (C) Sufficiency hardening worth adding while touching the gate: require `!s.coasting || s.deceleration_rad_s2 > 0. || s.target_rate_rad_s == 0.` (or simply deceleration > 0 whenever |target_rate| > 0) so the coast servo cannot be parked in an undecaying spin; bound `s.cadence_ema` to a physically reachable band (e.g. <= a few hundred rpm) since a bogus EMA buys phantom cadence-coast time proportional to coast_cadence_tau_s; mirror these in decode_policies or document explicitly that the codec is syntactic-only and set_state is the semantic gate. (D) Restate clause 1 on BOTH sequences - `|effort_k - effort_{k-1}| <= effort_slew_nm_s * dt` for consecutive PedalMode::pedaling returns AND for consecutive committed state_.effort values across update() calls - so the phantom-baseline class (mid-update state zeroing) is covered unambiguously, and explicitly note that externally injected states via set_state are constrained by clause 3 to the reset-reachable domain where the two readings coincide.
